CREATE TABLE `isu` (
  `id` bigint AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `jia_isu_uuid` CHAR(36) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  `name` VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  `image` LONGBLOB,
  `character` VARCHAR(255),
  `jia_user_id` VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  `created_at` DATETIME(6) DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  `updated_at` DATETIME(6) DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
   PRIMARY KEY(`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARACTER SET=utf8mb4\G
# Converted for EXPLAIN
# EXPLAIN /*!50100 PARTITIONS*/
select =utf8mb4 from CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
   PRIMARY KEY(`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARACTER \G

CREATE TABLE `isu_condition` (
  `id` bigint AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `jia_isu_uuid` CHAR(36) NOT NULL,
  `timestamp` DATETIME NOT NULL,
  `is_sitting` TINYINT(1) NOT NULL,
  `condition` VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  `message` VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,

  PRIMARY KEY(`id`),
  INDEX `idx_uuid_timestamp` (`jia_isu_uuid`, `timestamp`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARACTER SET=utf8mb4\G
